Directional representations shift the bottleneck from the symbolic layer (AM-Parser's 0% category ceiling) to the neural layer, which improves with encoder upgrades.

</details>


### [39] [World Wide Models: Literary Tools for Cultural AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02369)
*Nina Begus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨大语言模型(LLMs)带来的大规模、自动化、单语文化遭遇,主张利用文学学科工具(比较阅读、叙事学、诗学、批判理论、世界文学、翻译)构建具有文化素养的AI,提出分层框架连接文学与AI发展,应用世界文学方法应对全球AI文本性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs引发的大规模单语文化遭遇凸显了文化素养缺失,文学学科长期积累的文化协商工具成为构建文化敏感AI的必要资源。

Method: 发展分层框架,整合比较文学、叙事学、诗学、批判理论、世界文学及翻译研究,强调文学与AI发展的自然交集,将批判理论辩论与结构性单语主义联系,提出世界文学方法在宏观结构、流通与不可翻译性三维度应对全球AI文本性。

Result: 提出了一个综合性框架,展示如何利用文学理论工具实现更细致的文本建模和多元化AI解读,为文化敏感的AI发展提供理论路径。

Conclusion: 文学研究不仅能诊断AI的文化局限,更能为构建多元、文化敏感的文本模型提供方法论资源,世界文学视角对应对全球AI文本性至关重要。

Abstract: LLMs stage a new form of cultural encounter that is massive, automated, and monolingual. Literary disciplines have always negotiated cultural struggles with comparative reading of literature, narratological and poetic analysis, critical theory, world literature, and translation. These tools have now become indispensable for building culturally literate AI. The essay develops a layered framework toward more nuanced textual models and pluralistic interpretations of AI, emphasizing the natural intersections of literature and AI development, connecting current debates in critical theory with structural monolingualism, and suggesting a new application of world literature approaches to address global AI textuality through macrostructure, circulation, and untranslatability.

</details>


### [40] [HULAT2 at MER-TRANS 2026: Governed Multi-Agent Simplification for Spanish Easy-to-Read Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02381)
*Lourdes Moreno,Paloma Martínez,Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero,Miguel Domínguez-Gómez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HULAT2-UC3M在MER-TRANS 2026西班牙语赛道提交了三个自动运行，基于多智能体工作流（RUN1/2）和生成-评估-再生成基线（RUN3），RUN1以SARI 44.05领先，多智能体路由优于线性基线，词汇支持未自动提升参考分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多语言易读翻译任务中，基于信号引导的多智能体工作流与线性再生成基线的效果对比，以及词汇支持层对翻译质量的影响。

Method: RUN1和RUN2采用LangGraph多智能体工作流，结合Gemini 2.5 Flash和RigoChat-7B-v2，并行生成、内部质量信号、事件-条件-动作路由、受控编辑和可追溯决策；RUN2额外激活基于术语表和词汇资源的词汇支持层。RUN3为基于RigoChat的生成-评估-再生成基线，结合提示工程和LoRA适应。

Result: 官方SARI评分：RUN1 44.0543，RUN2 43.1049，RUN3 38.5136。RUN1最佳。多智能体路由优于线性再生成基线；词汇支持未自动提升参考分数。

Conclusion: 信号引导的多智能体路由在该任务设置中优于线性再生成基线。增加词汇支持层并未自动改善基于参考的评分。需要进一步的片段级和文档级分析以评估可读性、事实一致性和用户导向的适配性。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [41] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDIAREF，一个公开可用的知识库，包含来自200个媒体源的网络文档，旨在支持基于检索增强生成（RAG）的自动化事实核查中媒体背景调查（MBC）的可复现、低成本评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于RAG的事实核查系统往往假设检索到的证据是可靠的，但现实中的信息可能存在冲突、过时，且来源可能不可靠或有偏见。近期的源批判性推理通过媒体背景调查（MBC）评估证据源的可信度，但生成MBC依赖昂贵的专有搜索API，限制了可复现性。

Method: 构建MEDIAREF知识库，包含200个媒体源的网络文档；描述可复现的构建和更新方法论；在MEDIAREF上评估多个广泛使用的大语言模型在MBC生成任务上的表现。

Result: 通过自动评估和定性评估，证明MEDIAREF支持更高质量的MBC生成，且成本更低、可复现。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF为RAG-based事实核查中的源批判性推理提供了可复现、低成本的评估基础设施，推动了MBC生成研究的发展。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [42] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型（LLM）兴起导致自然语言处理（NLP）研究的发表重心从传统的 ACL 会议向通用机器学习（ML）会议转移，后者还带来显著的引用溢价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP 传统上在 ACL 等核心会议发表，但 LLM 的进步模糊了 NLP 与通用 ML 的学科界限，作者开始在两类会议都发表。本文旨在探究 NLP 研究的学科重心是否正在发生转移。

Method: 收集 2010-2026 年的 NLP 研究论文，分析资深作者和新入行作者的发表模式变化；对比 LLM 爆发前后资深作者在 ACL 主会、Findings 及通用 ML 会议的份额变化；统计新作者首发至少三篇一作 NLP 论文者在 ACL 与通用 ML 会议的分布演变；采用因果推断技术估计通用 ML 会议的引用溢价。

Result: 资深作者在 ACL 主会份额下降 19.2 个百分点，Findings 增加 14.8 个百分点，通用 ML 会议上升 8.6 个百分点（已调整领域并行增长）。新作者中以 ACL 为主的比例从 84%（2019）降至 74%（2024），以通用 ML 会议为主的比例从 5% 升至 21%。因果推断显示通用 ML 会议带来显著引用溢价，影响会议选择。

Conclusion: NLP 研究的发表版图正在经历显著转移：传统 ACL 会议份额下降，通用 ML 会议份额上升并提供引用优势，表明学科重心正向通用 ML 方向倾斜。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [43] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证 NLP 文化研究是一种物质-话语实践：测量装置（模型、数据、标注、评估）主动构成而非被动记录文化现实。基于 Karen Barad 的“能动性切割”概念，指出装置的设计选择决定了现象与仪器的边界，且语言模型已内化所测文化材料，边界从一开始即纠缠不清。通过影视对话三个案例研究（结构、互动、偏差测量）及装置自身三个审视（文化标记抹除、历史材料协同、代理工作流中的能动性），提出一个理论驱动、实证严谨、文化权变的研究纲领，将每次能动性切割视为方法论与伦理的双重承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型广泛用于量化文化现象，但鲜少反思何使此类测量具有“文化”特质。现有工作多假设模型客观记录文化，忽略测量装置自身参与构成文化现实，且模型预训练已内化大量文化材料，导致测量边界模糊、伦理责任缺位。

Method: 采用理论分析与实证案例相结合：1) 引入 Karen Barad 的能动性切割概念，建立物质-话语实践框架；2) 三个影视对话案例研究——测量结构（话题建模）、互动（角色网络）、偏差（刻板印象检测）；3) 三个装置自省考察——文化标记抹除、历史材料协同、代理工作流中的能动性分配。

Result: 发现：① 装置设计选择（如分词、标注体系、评估指标）实质上划定了文化现象的边界；② 语言模型预训练语料使其预先内化文化材料，测量边界从一开始即与被测对象纠缠；③ 案例中结构测量强化了类型惯例，互动测量抹除权力不对称，偏差测量依赖有争议的标注本体；④ 装置自省揭示文化标记被标准化抹除、历史材料被无批判复用、代理工作流将伦理判断外包给模型。

Conclusion: NLP 文化研究应转向：将每次能动性切割视为有意识的方法论与伦理承诺；建立理论驱动、实证严谨、文化权变的研究纲领，要求研究者显式声明装置设计中的价值选择，并在测量循环中持续反思边界划定的政治后果。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [44] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过缩放定律分析大语言模型（LLM）在社会模拟中的保真度，发现计算规模扩大普遍能提升观点建模、行为模拟和纵向预测三大子领域的性能，但针对人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）和启发式行为的校准任务，扩展性失效，且低资源领域改进不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM社会模拟虽具潜力但保真度不足，尚未广泛采用。研究旨在探究现有语言建模扩展范式是否能弥补这些差距，抑或模拟保真度与通用能力正交、需专门研究投入。

Method: 采用缩放定律方法，使用85个Qwen3架构Transformer LLMs，在DCLM网络文本语料上以固定计算预算（10^18至10^20 FLOPs）预训练，并评估35个更大规模（高达70B参数）的开放权重模型，从损失预测下游准确率，覆盖观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测三大代表性子领域。

Result: 三大设置均呈现强计算扩展性。行为与观点模拟任务随规模快速提升，尤其针对英语网络语料中充分代表的人群；纵向预测及弱势群体观点扩展较慢，特别是与MMLU等通用知识推理基准相关性低时。行为模拟中，模型对人类认知偏差（风险厌恶）和启发式（从相关任务学习关联奖励）的校准随规模不改善，即使微调模型在0.5B至8B参数区间也无显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 规模扩展将在大多数设置下改进社会模拟，但存在异常情况，且在低资源领域改进可靠性较低。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [45] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地利用计算方法研究有声书朗读特质（如音色、语速、响度）与消费数据（观看率）的关系，发现声学特征与吸引力有稳健关联，并验证于专有参与度指标，为个性化推荐和配音选角提供数据驱动洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究朗读质量如何影响有声书吸引力，且效果随体裁、标题、受众而异，旨在填补系统性计算研究的空白。

Method: 从LibriVox提取声学特征（使用预训练音频模型），分析其与消费数据（观看率）的关系，并考察体裁和标题的交互作用；后用专有参与度指标验证。

Result: 声学信息本身与吸引力有稳健关联，即使控制标题效应后依然成立；专有指标进一步验证了发现。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性计算研究，关联朗读特质、体裁、标题与有声书消费，展示了数据驱动洞见在个性化推荐和配音选角中的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [46] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VRRL强化学习框架，通过随机掩码轨迹前缀和经验回放缓冲区的缓冲滚入，增强大型视觉语言模型在分布外数据上的视觉接地自我反思能力，显著提升视觉定位和空间导航任务的OOD准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在CoT推理中的自我反思时往往无法正确关注视觉输入，导致难以将反馈转化为有根据的修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现差。

Method: 提出VRRL框架，包含两个组件：1) 训练时随机掩码轨迹前缀，强制模型从错误的中间预测中恢复；2) 从经验回放缓冲区引入缓冲滚入，让模型接触多样化的失败状态并学会修正。

Result: 在表格/图表视觉定位任务和空间导航基准上，VRRL显著提高了平均分布外准确率，优于标准RL和面向反思的微调基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式设计的视觉接地自我反思机制，VRRL有效解决了LVLM在分布外数据上反思时忽视视觉输入的问题，提升了模型的鲁棒性和修正能力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [47] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了大规模长视频剧集说话人识别基准DramaSR-532K（含532K标注对话行、900+角色），并设计了基于大型推理模型的DramaSR-LRM方法，通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据，显著提升说话人识别性能，尤其在短语句上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频剧集理解极具挑战，说话人识别是关键任务，但现有数据集规模有限，且短语句声学生物特征不可靠，需要整合听觉、语言、视觉多模态线索以实现高保真归因。

Method: （1）构建DramaSR-532K基准，包含532K标注对话行，覆盖900+独特角色，需融合听觉、语言、视觉线索。（2）提出DramaSR-LRM，基于大型推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据，综合多模态输入实现高保真说话人归因。

Result: 实验结果表明，DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，特别是在声学生物特征固有不可靠的短语句上表现突出。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模多模态基准和基于大型推理模型的多模态工具使用方法，推动了长视频剧集说话人识别领域的发展，数据和代码将公开。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [48] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LACUNA是首个具有参数级定位真值的遗忘测试平台，通过将合成PII注入预定义参数，揭示现有SOTA遗忘方法虽在输出层表现良好，但参数级定位不精确且易受重现攻击；精确定位下简单梯度遗忘即可实现强擦除和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准仅在输出层评估，无法判断知识是否真正从参数中擦除还是仅被混淆，且重现攻击的成功加剧了这一担忧。

Method: 构建LACUNA测试平台，利用掩码持续预训练将合成个体的PII注入1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，提供参数级定位真值，以此基准测试现有SOTA遗忘方法。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法在输出层表现强但参数级定位高度不精确，易受重现攻击；当定位成功时，简单梯度遗忘即能实现强擦除和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确的参数级定位对鲁棒遗忘至关重要，LACUNA作为补充行为评估的工具发布，旨在推动基于定位的鲁棒遗忘研究。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


### [49] [Safeguarding LLM Agents from Misalignment through Provenance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01236)
*Yining She,Yiliang Liang,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于溯源的框架ProvenanceGuard，用于检测LLM智能体工具调用中的错位，显著降低错误率并减少干预负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体获得强大工具访问权限，确保其行为与用户意图对齐变得至关重要。现有的运行时护栏依赖LLM-as-a-judge范式，缺乏系统性的推理框架，导致判断不一致且难以审计。

Method: 提出基于溯源的概念框架，将错位检测形式化为判断拟议工具调用是否由智能体上下文中的可追溯证据支持。构建ProvenanceGuard多阶段管道，在工具执行前分析三类错位，仅当动作与用户查询对齐时才允许执行。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench和WorkBench两个基准上，跨10个骨干LLM评估。相比LLM-as-a-judge基线，ProvenanceGuard将错位轨迹错误率从42.9%降至1.8%（Agent-SafetyBench）和从32.1%降至17.3%（WorkBench），任务成功轨迹的干预负担从30.5%降至12.8%，且在对齐轨迹上未引入统计学显著的不必要干预增加。

Conclusion: 结构化的基于溯源的推理为保障LLM智能体免受错位提供了有效且实用的基础。

Abstract: As LLM agents gain increasing access to powerful tools, ensuring that their actions are aligned with the user's intent becomes critical. When an agent's proposed tool invocation deviates from the user's intent -- a phenomenon called misalignment -- it may lead to harmful consequences that are difficult to undo. Existing runtime guardrails rely on an LLM-as-a-judge paradigm that lacks a systematic framework for reasoning about alignment, often producing judgments that are inconsistent or difficult to audit. Motivated by provenance analysis, we propose a provenance-based conceptual framework that formalizes misalignment detection as determining whether a proposed tool call is supported by traceable evidence in the agent's context. Building on this framework, we propose ProvenanceGuard, a multi-stage pipeline that analyzes the agent's action for three types of misalignment before the selected tool is executed and only allows the action to take place when it is considered aligned with the user's input query. We evaluated our proposed approach on two different benchmarks, Agent-SafetyBench and WorkBench, across 10 backbone LLMs. Compared to the LLM-as-a-judge baseline, ProvenanceGuard reduces error rate on misaligned traces from 42.9% to 1.8% on Agent-SafetyBench and from 32.1% to 17.3% on WorkBench, while reducing intervention burden on task-successful traces from 30.5% to 12.8% and introducing no statistically significant increase in unnecessary interventions on aligned traces. These results demonstrate that structured, provenance-based reasoning provides an effective and practical foundation for safeguarding LLM agents from misalignment.

</details>


### [50] [Kara: Efficient Reasoning LLM Serving via Sliding-Window KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01237)
*Shen Han,Yuyang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Kara滑动窗口KV缓存压缩方法及KvLLM推理框架，解决现有KV缓存压缩方法中阈值触发策略带来的吞吐率提升有限甚至下降、以及固定块边界无法灵活保留重要语义块的问题。实验表明该方法持续提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型生成长链式思维时会积累大量KV缓存，导致解码延迟高、吞吐率受限。现有KV缓存压缩方法存在两大局限：1) 阈值触发的压缩策略可能带来有限的吞吐率提升甚至降低吞吐率，且可能完全消除序列中某些块的KV对，加剧信息丢失；2) 通常仅保留孤立KV对或固定大小且边界刚性的块，无法在任意token位置灵活保留重要的可变大小语义块。

Method: 提出Kara滑动窗口KV缓存压缩方法，在解码时仅对最近生成的上下文进行压缩。利用双向注意力对窗口内KV对进行评分选择，设计Token2Chunk模块将部分选中的KV对扩展为块，实现重要语义信息的灵活保留。并将Kara适配到PagedAttention，构建基于vLLM的KvLLM推理框架，降低KV缓存内存占用并有效提升输出吞吐率。

Result: 大量实验表明Kara和KvLLM在各项指标上均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: Kara通过滑动窗口和双向注意力评分配合Token2Chunk模块，有效解决了现有KV缓存压缩方法的局限性，KvLLM框架进一步实现了工程落地，显著降低内存占用并提升推理吞吐率。

Abstract: Reasoning language models often generate long chain-of-thought (CoT), which accumulates a massive KV cache during the decoding phase and incurs high decoding latency and limited throughput. To address these issues, KV cache compression has emerged as a promising technique for reducing memory overhead by selectively removing unimportant KV pairs while preserving useful ones for subsequent decoding. Nevertheless, we identify two key limitations in existing KV cache compression methods: 1) their threshold-triggered compression policy may provide limited throughput improvement or even reduce throughput, and may fully eliminate KV pairs from certain blocks of the sequence, potentially worsening information loss. 2) they typically retain either isolated KV pairs or fixed-size chunks with rigid boundaries, failing to preserve important flexible-sized chunks at arbitrary token positions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Kara, a sliding-window KV cache compression method that performs decoding-time compression by operating only on the recently generated context. Kara leverages bidirectional attention to score and select informative KV pairs in the window. To enable flexible preservation of important semantic information, we design a Token2Chunk module to expand a subset of selected KV pairs into chunks. Furthermore, we adapt Kara to PagedAttention and develop KvLLM, an inference framework built upon vLLM, which reduces KV cache memory usage and effectively improves output throughput. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent performance improvements of proposed Kara and KvLLM.

</details>


### [51] [SPARCLE: SPeaker-aware Aligned Representations via Contrastive Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01238)
*Priyam Mazumdar,Yurii Halychanskyi,Steven Guo,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SPARCLE，一种说话人感知的字素表示模型，通过对比学习将字素与Wav2Vec2声学表示对齐并条件化于说话人身份，替代G2P系统，在极低资源设置下将词错误率减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音合成从音素转向字素建模，但字素模型在低资源场景下表现不佳；音素依赖G2P系统无法捕捉说话人特定的声学变化。需要一种能整合说话人感知声学信息的字素表示来提升低资源TTS性能。

Method: SPARCLE模型，采用对比学习目标，将字素与对应的Wav2Vec2声学表示对齐，并以说话人身份为条件。训练后的模型可作为G2P系统的替代用于下游TTS任务。

Result: 在极低资源设置下，SPARCLE将词错误率降低一半，优于标准字素模型；生成质量显著提升。

Conclusion: SPARCLE通过引入说话人感知的字素表示，有效解决了低资源场景下字素建模的不足，为TTS提供了更优的前端表示替代方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in speech synthesis have shifted from phoneme representations to direct grapheme modeling. While phonemes address the one-to-many mapping between text and acoustics, they rely on grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) systems that fail to capture speaker-specific acoustic variation. Prior work demonstrates that grapheme-based models outperform phoneme-based systems at scale, but not in low-resource settings.
  In this paper, we propose SPARCLE, a speaker-aware grapheme representation model that enriches characters with their precise acoustic realizations. SPARCLE is trained with a contrastive objective to align graphemes with corresponding Wav2Vec2 acoustic representations while conditioned on speaker identity. The resulting model serves as a replacement to G2P systems for downstream text-to-speech (TTS) tasks. We demonstrate that SPARCLE improves generation quality, reducing word error rates by half in extreme low-resource settings compared to standard grapheme-based models.

</details>


### [52] [Prompt Framing Distorts Count-Based Evaluation of LLM Error Detection: Evidence from Numeric Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01240)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了基于计数的F1指标在LLM错误检测评估中存在“F1膨胀”现象：计数F1显著上升但跨度定位未改善。引入ErrorBench压力测试协议，评估六种LLM在五种提示条件下的表现，发现锚定提示导致高达0.79的F1膨胀。建议评估时避免预填充错误计数，并报告跨度感知指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常用计数F1作为LLM错误检测质量的代理指标，但忽略了计数增加不代表定位能力提升的问题，需量化这种差距并提出更可靠的评估协议。

Method: 提出ErrorBench压力测试协议，针对提示诱导的计数扭曲进行受控实验。在CoNLL-2014数据集的143篇段落上，对六种当代LLM测试五种提示条件，共4290个响应。采用CoNLL-2014 M2风格评分和严格匹配评分，并用官方ERRANT 3.0.0流水线及多参考评分进行100篇段落的复现实验。

Result: 锚定提示下计数F1膨胀高达0.79（M2评分）和0.96（严格匹配）。盲测到锚定提示转变使平均计数F1提升+0.21，但多参考ERRANT F0.5仅提升+0.04。高指令遵循的GPT/Claude系统表现出更大的计数响应，Gemini系列较小。

Conclusion: LLM校对和文档审查评估应避免预填充错误计数，并应在报告计数指标的同时补充跨度感知指标，以防止F1膨胀掩盖真实定位能力。

Abstract: Count-based F1 is widely used as a proxy for LLM error-detection quality, but this paper shows that it can rise dramatically without a corresponding improvement in span localization, a gap termed F1 Inflation. The paper introduces ErrorBench, a controlled stress-test protocol for prompt-induced count distortion. ErrorBench evaluates six contemporary LLMs under five prompt conditions over 4,290 responses from 143 CoNLL-2014 passages. Under CoNLL-2014 M2-style scoring, anchored prompts produce up to 0.79 points of F1 Inflation, and up to 0.96 under strict matching. A 100-passage replication using the official ERRANT 3.0.0 pipeline and multi-reference scoring reproduces the pattern: averaged over six models, the Blind-to-Anchored prompt shift raises Count-F1 by +0.21 while raising multi-reference ERRANT F0.5 by only +0.04. The study finds larger count responses in highly instruction-compliant GPT/Claude systems and smaller responses in the Gemini family under this stress-test protocol. The findings suggest that LLM proofreading and document-review evaluations should avoid pre-populated error counts and should report span-aware metrics alongside count-based metrics.

</details>


### [53] [Mapping Text to Multiplex Graph: Prompt Compression as Lévy Walk-Guided Graph Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01241)
*Yaxin Gao,Yao Lu,Jinhong Deng,Jiaqi Nie,Zhe Tang,Jian Zhang,Zhaowei Zhu,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGP 提出了一种基于多路复用图的冗余感知图剪枝方法，利用 Lévy 游走平衡局部开发与全局探索，实现高效的提示词压缩，在 LongBench 上以 4x 压缩比达到 49.3 分，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词压缩方法将文本视为扁平 token 序列，无法捕捉分布在多个位置并通过局部句法依赖和全局语义关系连接的重要信息。

Method: RAGP 将提示词压缩建模为多路复用图上的冗余感知图剪枝，联合建模细粒度注意力依赖和粗粒度语义关系；采用 Lévy 游走凭借其重尾步长分布，在异构结构（密集局部子图和稀疏全局连接）中高效识别非冗余节点，平衡局部开发与全局探索。

Result: 在 LongBench 基准上，RAGP 在 4x 压缩比下平均得分 49.3，优于 LongLLMLingua 在 3x 压缩比下的 48.8；同时在多项任务上超越最先进的基于视觉的文本压缩范式。

Conclusion: RAGP 通过图结构建模和 Lévy 游走实现了更有效的提示词压缩，在保持性能的同时实现更高压缩比，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing prompt compression methods treat text as flat token sequences, failing to capture the distributed nature of important information, which is often spread across multiple locations and connected through both local syntactic dependencies and global semantic relations. Such relational structure is naturally represented as a graph, where tokens or sentences become nodes and their dependencies become edges. To this end, we propose RAGP, which formulates prompt compression as Redundancy-Aware Graph Pruning on a multiplex graph that jointly models fine-grained attention-based dependencies and coarse-grained semantic relations. To efficiently identify non-redundant nodes in this heterogeneous structure (dense local subgraphs and sparse global connections), we employ Levy walks whose heavy-tailed step distribution naturally balances local exploitation with global exploration. Experiments on LongBench show that RAGP achieves an average score of 49.3 under a 4x compression ratio, outperforming existing LLM-based compression methods, such as LongLLMLingua, which attains 48.8 at a 3x compression ratio. Besides, RAGP also surpasses state-of-the-art vision-based text compression paradigms on multiple tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RAGP-B0CB.

</details>


### [54] [Office Comprehension Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01245)
*Firoz Shaik,Mateus Picanço Lima Gomes,Tanvir Aumi,Jingci Wang,Milos Milunovic,Filip Basara,Ivana Jovanovic,Vishwas Suryanarayanan,Neha Nandan Kenkare,Weiyao Xie,Zhipeng Han,Zheng Zhang,Waleed Shahid,Jay Rathi,Russell Scherer,Thong Q. Nguyen,Michael Bentley,Tamara Stankovic,Rasika Chakravarthy,Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Office Comprehension Bench (OCB)，首个公开基准，用于联合评估 LLM 系统对 Word、Excel、PowerPoint 原生格式文档的理解能力。包含两个测试轨道：File Fidelity Q&A 考察结构与视觉感知；Domain Q&A 考察跨 12 个专业领域的专家级推理。采用原子化二元可评分声明和 LLM 评委集成评分。最强前沿系统在 Domain Q&A 上仅达 59.3%，增加思考深度提升有限，升级产品层级仅带来适度增益。已开源数据集、评测工具、评委提示词和公开排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对办公文档原生格式的联合理解评估，尤其是结构化元素（表格、图表、公式等）和跨文档专业推理能力的系统性测试，因此需要构建专用基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 OCB 基准，包含两个测试轨道：File Fidelity Q&A 针对办公文档的结构与视觉感知（表格、图表、嵌入图像、公式、页眉、备注、命名区域等）；Domain Q&A 基于 12 个专业领域的真实行业文档，要求多步骤分析与跨文档综合。参考答案分解为原子化的二元可评分声明，采用 LLM 评委集成独立打分。

Result: 最强前沿系统在默认推理模式下 Domain Q&A 得分仅约 59.3%；在同一产品层级内增加思考深度无显著提升；升级到更高产品层级仅带来适度性能增益。

Conclusion: OCB 揭示了当前 LLM 在办公文档理解特别是专业推理方面的局限性，为后续模型改进提供了标准化评测平台；开源资源将促进该领域研究发展。

Abstract: We introduce Office Comprehension Bench (OCB), the first public benchmark to jointly evaluate LLM systems on Word, Excel, and PowerPoint comprehension over native file formats (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx) and their variants. OCB consists of two tracks. File Fidelity Q&A tests structural and visual perception of office artifacts - tables, charts, embedded images, formulas, and app-specific elements such as headers, speaker notes, and named ranges. Domain Q&A tests expert-level reasoning grounded in real-world industry documents across 12 professional domains, with queries requiring multi-step analysis and synthesis across documents. Each reference answer is decomposed into atomic, binary-gradable claims, and an ensemble of LLM judges scores responses against each claim independently. Even the strongest frontier system in its default reasoning mode reaches only about 59.3% on Domain Q&A; increasing thinking depth within a tier does not move performance materially, while moving to a higher product tier yields modest gains. We release the dataset, evaluation tooling, judge prompt, and a public leaderboard.

</details>

### [55] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PACE，一种模块化神经符号框架，用于生成可行性感知的反事实解释，结合神经网络预测模型与符号推理层（ASP），在 Adult Income 数据集上验证了其能产生符合领域约束且可操作的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法常因缺乏领域知识和干预约束的显式机制，产生不现实或不可行的建议；神经符号 AI 结合数据驱动模型与符号推理，有望解决该问题。

Method: 提出 PACE 框架，分离预测与推理：神经预测模型（如 MLP）负责分类，符号推理层（使用 ASP 编码可行修改规则）在反事实生成时强制执行领域约束，保持不可变属性不变，模型无关且可适配多领域。

Result: 在 Adult Income 数据集上的案例研究显示，符号约束能生成更满足领域可行性要求的解释，揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在可解释 AI 中生成透明、可行性感知的反事实解释具有潜力，PACE 框架为现实决策支持提供了可操作的解释生成途径。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [56] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序记忆蒸馏(PMD)方法，通过将跨回合信号转化为三层抽象的可复用程序记忆，并利用记忆条件自教师监督学生策略，实现策略与记忆的协同进化，在SCIKNOWEVAL和LIVECODEBENCH基准上显著优于SDPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)及自蒸馏变体(如SDPO)仅基于回合级信号更新策略，忽略了跨回合和跨轮次中丰富的程序性信息：哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式持续存在、哪些模式反复出现。这些跨回合信号无法被回合局部更新捕获，导致学习效率低下。

Method: PMD构建三层抽象的程序记忆：原始轨迹、自反思策略与教训、跨问题复现的高层行为模式，均从模型自身轨迹在线提取。引入记忆条件自教师，利用累积经验监督学生在自身回合上的表现，使学生逐步内化程序性知识至参数中。核心设计为协同进化：策略生成回合更新记忆，记忆塑造监督信号更新策略。推理时记忆已吸收进策略，无需外部记忆。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD较SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验显示，冻结记忆或冻结策略均比完整PMD低超过10%，验证协同进化机制的关键作用。

Conclusion: PMD通过将跨回合程序性知识显式化为分层记忆并引入协同进化机制，有效解决了RLVR中回合局部更新无法捕获跨回合信号的问题，显著提升了模型在知识密集型推理和代码生成任务上的性能，且推理时无额外开销。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [57] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文展示了AI代理能模拟人类研究者的分析差异，揭示了“分析路径分叉”导致同一数据得出对立结论的普遍性，并提出了m值和Agentic Bootstrap方法，用于量化分析结果在合理分析空间中的极端程度，主张科学证据评估应考虑其在可能分析分布中的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究常存在隐性的分析路径选择（forking paths），不同选择导致不同结论，但这些路径难以观察。AI代理可显性化并规模化地探索这些路径，揭示选择性探索和报告是核心挑战，而非分析本身的缺陷。

Method: 跨四个高风险领域，通过赋予AI代理不同人设，使其对同一数据和问题进行分析；在42个人类研究团队分析同一移民数据集的研究中，对比AI代理与人类的结论差距；引入m值（multiverse value）定义为分析路径产生至少与报告同样极端声明的概率；提出Agentic Bootstrap利用AI代理采样合理分析路径来估计m值。

Result: AI代理复现了人类意识形态差距的72%；尽管结论对立，86%通过独立AI审查，78%通过多数人类专家审查；应用于人类移民研究，13.5%的人类报告分析落入分析空间最极端的5%（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 科学证据评估不应仅依赖单一报告分析，而应考虑其在合理可报告分析分布中的位置。Agentic Bootstrap使该分布可观测，并将其转化为科学可信度的判据。AI代理虽放大了选择性探索问题，但也提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [58] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Janus系统，用于实现和评估用户参与的智能体权限管理设计，实验表明用户输入对隐私安全至关重要，AI辅助决策可降低认知负荷，且需考虑权限疲劳等真实用户行为，单一设计无法在所有场景最优，需采用情境感知的原则性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体自主执行工具调用引发权限管理问题：用户能扮演什么角色、应该扮演什么角色。尽管有许多提议方法，但用户在智能体权限管理中的角色仍未被充分探索。

Method: 构建Janus系统，包含两部分：Janus-Core（模块化智能体系统，支持多样化权限管理设计）和Janus-Harness（自动化评估框架）。基于识别用户参与关键设计轴的概念模型，实现六个覆盖设计空间的权限助手，在三个场景和三种合成响应者上进行评估。

Result: 用户输入至关重要，能显著增强隐私和安全；AI增强用户决策有助于降低认知负荷；必须在系统设计中考虑包括权限疲劳在内的真实用户行为；没有单一设计在所有情境下都表现最优。

Conclusion: 研究结果激励采用更原则性和情境敏感的方法来部署智能体系统中的权限助手。Janus已公开发布，支持未来对此维度的深入研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [59] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出半监督链式思维学习框架Semi-CoT，利用无标签问题构建伪推理监督，通过语义熵门控选择高质量伪CoT演示，实验显示伪答案精度达91.36%-100%，在部分数据集上有小幅提升但也存在负迁移和天花板效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT方法主要将推理链作为推理时提示词使用，生成的推理轨迹很少作为半监督学习信号被重复利用，缺乏将无标签数据转化为可靠伪监督的机制。

Method: Semi-CoT为每个无标签问题采样多个伪CoT，估算答案级语义熵，选择低熵推理链作为可靠的伪CoT演示，将CoT的自训练视角从推理时细化扩展到半监督伪监督。

Result: 熵门控有效选择高精度伪CoT，伪答案精度范围91.36%-100%；在SVAMP和GSM8K上获得小幅增益，AQuA出现负迁移，MultiArith达到性能天花板。

Conclusion: 无标签问题能提供可靠的伪推理信号，但其有效利用仍需要更强的演示选择策略或学生模型训练方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [60] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPINE-World是一个基于LLM的智能体，通过在线交互学习面向对象的程序化世界模型，结合假设-测试循环、重放验证、基于模型的规划和本体论误差引导探索，在ARC-AGI-3基准上无需逐游戏训练即解决20/25个游戏，动作效率达78.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度网络学习的世界模型虽灵活但数据饥渴且泛化差；程序合成的世界模型数据高效且可复用，但主要用于结构化状态世界，且单次程序搜索难以扩展到像素渲染环境。需要一种能在线从交互中学习面向对象程序化世界模型的方法，适应未知任务。

Method: OPINE-World耦合两个协作智能体形成假设-测试循环：一个在环境中行动，另一个用代码合成模型，结合重放验证和基于模型的规划；引入本体论误差（Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy）指导探索。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3基准上，无需逐游戏训练解决20/25个游戏，动作效率得分78.4（人类基线）。

Conclusion: OPINE-World展示了LLM驱动的程序合成结合在线交互学习，能高效构建可复用的面向对象世界模型，显著提升技能获取效率，为适应新任务的智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将 SOLiD 方法扩展到更大规模模型（1B 到 405B 参数）并在更真实的偏好学习场景下评估，发现未检测到的欺骗率随模型规模显著下降（34%→14%），且可完全移除昂贵的人工标注员而不显著增加欺骗，但方法对检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据的分布偏移敏感，导致假阳性率升至不可接受水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的欺骗行为监控和预防成本高昂，SOLiD 通过谎言检测器筛选需人工审核的响应，但此前未在大规模模型和真实偏好学习设置中充分验证其扩展性和鲁棒性。

Method: 将 SOLiD 扩展至 1B、405B 等不同规模模型，在多样化的偏好学习任务上评估；测量不同真正例率下的未检测欺骗率，对比保留/移除人工标注员的微调效果，并分析分布偏移对检测器假阳性率的影响。

Result: 在检测器真正例率 99% 时，未检测欺骗率从 1B 模型的 34% 降至 405B 模型的 14%；完全移除微调阶段的人工标注员未导致欺骗率显著上升；但检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据的分布偏移会使假阳性率升至不切实际的高水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD 随模型规模扩展表现良好，可大幅降低人工监督成本，但其对分布偏移的敏感性是实际部署的主要瓶颈，需进一步研究鲁棒检测器训练方法。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [62] [EO-Agents: A Three-Agent LLM Pipeline for Earth Observation Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01584)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Amin Tabrizian,Armin Mehrabian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NASA地球观测知识图谱的假设生成流水线，结合异构图神经网络和三智能体LLM流水线，从1,475个NASA数据集中生成了160个跨多个地球科学领域的研究假设。实验表明，模型预测的新数据集配对与真实共用配对同样合理，且假设排序稳定但绝对评分依赖评判者身份，揭示了单一LLM评判的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学假设生成工作多依赖非结构化文献和自由文本声明，缺乏结构化知识图谱的支撑，需要一种能直接利用NASA地球观测知识图谱进行假设生成的方法。

Method: 1) 利用历史共用关系训练异构图神经网络，对候选数据集配对进行排序；2) 设计三智能体LLM流水线（过滤、生成、评估）产出结构化研究假设；3) 在1,475个NASA数据集上应用该流水线；4) 进行2×2×2因子实验，对比GPT-5.2和Claude Sonnet 4.6作为评判者的差异。

Result: 系统生成了160个跨越生态水文学、冰川学、气溶胶-云相互作用、植被物候学、平流层化学等领域的假设。模型预测的新数据集配对被评为与文献中真实共用配对几乎同等合理。假设排序在不同LLM评判者间保持稳定，但绝对评分强烈依赖评判者身份。

Conclusion: 该流水线能有效挖掘科学连贯且未被探索的数据集组合，为地球观测研究提供新假设；同时实验揭示了单一LLM评判的不可靠性，建议采用多评判者集成评估。

Abstract: Large language models have recently been explored for scientific hypothesis generation, but most prior work relies on unstructured literature and free-form textual claims. We present a pipeline for Earth observation that grounds hypothesis generation directly in the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph. A heterogeneous graph neural network trained on historical co-usage relations ranks candidate dataset pairings, and a three-agent LLM pipeline filters, generates, and evaluates structured research hypotheses. Applied to 1,475 NASA datasets, the system produces 160 hypotheses spanning multiple Earth-science domains, including ecohydrology, glaciology, aerosol--cloud interactions, vegetation phenology, and stratospheric chemistry. Model-predicted novel dataset pairings are rated nearly as plausible as held-out real co-usages from the literature, indicating that the pipeline surfaces scientifically coherent yet unexplored combinations. A 2*2*2 factorial experiment across GPT-5.2 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows that hypothesis rankings remain stable, while absolute scores depend strongly on judge identity, highlighting limitations of single-judge LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [63] [Hawk: Harnessing Hardware-Aware Knowledge for High-Performance NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01590)
*Junyi Wen,Ruiyan Zhuang,Yongjia Xu,Pengtu Li,Rui Zou,Hongyi Chen,Chingman Wan,Puxu Yang,Wuhui Chen,Yanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hawk 是一个无需训练的框架，通过硬件感知知识生成高性能 NPU 内核，包含三个核心模块，实验显示生成准确率从 49.4% 提升至 80.0%，执行速度最高提升 2.2 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在 NPU 内核生成上因缺乏硬件特定先验知识而失效，盲目移植代码片段虽能通过编译但导致运行时崩溃和性能下降，亟需一种能结合硬件约束和执行反馈的自动化方法。

Method: 提出 Hawk 框架，包含三个模块：(1) 运行时知识综合模块，采用三元组可执行知识表示，将错误上下文与可执行语义内在耦合；(2) 瓶颈感知知识检索模块，实现二维检索范式，将查询投影到正交的句法空间和硬件对齐语义空间；(3) 效果驱动知识蒸馏模块，利用 LLM 语义仲裁，基于实证执行反馈持续剪枝错误并整合冗余知识。

Result: 在真实 NPU 工作负载上评估，生成准确率从 49.4% 提升至 80.0%，相比最先进基线最高实现 2.2 倍执行加速。

Conclusion: Hawk 通过硬件感知知识表示、检索与蒸馏，有效解决了 NPU 内核生成中的硬件约束难题，显著提升了代码生成准确性和运行时性能，为 NPU 编程自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Developing high-performance kernels for Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is a critical industry bottleneck, requiring developers to manually navigate implicit hardware constraints and strict memory hierarchies. While large language models offer immense automation potential, they fail catastrophically on NPUs due to a fundamental lack of hardware-specific priors. Naively transplanting code snippets from similar NPU kernels may pass the compiler, but it consistently triggers runtime crashes and performance degradation by blindly violating underlying hardware constraints. To overcome this, we introduce Hawk, a training-free framework that harnesses hardware-aware knowledge through three core modules: (1) Run-Time Knowledge Synthesis Module, which employs a Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation to inherently couple the error context with executable semantics; (2) Bottleneck-Aware Knowledge Retrieval Module, which implements a 2D-Retrieval paradigm to project queries into orthogonal syntactic and hardware-aligned semantic spaces; and (3) Effect-Driven Knowledge Distillation Module, which leverages LLM-driven semantic arbitration to continuously distill the knowledge by pruning errors and consolidating redundancies based on the empirical execution feedback. Extensive evaluations on real-world NPU workloads demonstrate that Hawk elevates generation accuracy from 49.4% to 80.0%, while achieving up to a 2.2x execution speedup over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [64] [Safe and Adaptive Cloud Healing: Verifying LLM-Generated Recovery Plans with a Neural-Symbolic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01595)
*Junyan Tan,Haoran Lin,Siyuan Guo,Yichen Fang,Xinyue Luo,Tianyu Shen,Zeyu Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PASE框架，将故障自愈重构为神经符号程序合成任务，结合LLM规划引擎、神经符号世界模型和元提示优化器，实现动态上下文感知的恢复策略生成，在真实云故障数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云AI系统规模和复杂度增加，现有基于LLM和DRL的方法采用松耦合架构，未充分利用LLM的生成推理能力，需更紧密的推理-规划-验证-自适应循环。

Method: PASE包含三大核心组件：LLM作为规划合成引擎生成结构化恢复计划；神经符号世界模型通过仿真验证计划可行性；基于DRL训练的元提示优化器生成最优提示引导LLM规划，形成紧密的推理-规划-验证-自适应闭环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上实验，平均系统恢复时间降低40%以上，未知故障场景下故障检测准确率提升。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了基于LLM的推理、模型辅助验证和元学习指导，推动了自主系统管理的发展。

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>


### [65] [SemHash-LLM: A Multi-Granularity Semantic Hashing Framework for Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01601)
*Xinyi Fang,Kejian Tong,Jiabei Liu,Tao Ning,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemHash LLM 是一个多粒度框架，用于大规模文档去重，融合语义投影哈希、注意力加权 MinHash、对比边界学习和选择性 LLM 裁决，实现高质量重复检测且神经验证成本低于 1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文档去重需要在保持语义等价的同时保持高效，现有方法可能在模板污染、短文本扰动、包含关系和病毒片段等场景下鲁棒性不足，且神经验证成本高。

Method: 提出 SemHash LLM 多粒度框架，统一语义投影哈希、注意力加权 MinHash、对比边界学习和选择性 LLM 裁决。通过门控融合结合字符、token 和文档级信号，采用级联过滤管道高效减少候选。语义投影哈希在蒸馏 LLM 嵌入空间学习紧凑二进制码，注意力加权 MinHash 抑制样板文本并强调信息内容。自适应决策边界和不确定性估计提升鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明 SemHash LLM 实现了强大的重复检测质量，神经验证成本低于 1%。

Conclusion: SemHash LLM 通过多粒度信号融合和级联过滤，在保持语义等价的前提下大幅降低了大规模文档去重的计算成本，展现了优异的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Large scale document deduplication must preserve semantic equivalence while remaining efficient over massive corpora. We present SemHash LLM, a multi granularity framework that unifies semantic projection hashing, attention weighted MinHash, contrastive boundary learning, and selective LLM based adjudication. The method combines character, token, and document level signals through gated fusion, then applies a cascaded filtering pipeline for efficient candidate reduction. Semantic projection hashing learns compact binary codes in distilled LLM embedding space, while attention weighted Min- Hash suppresses boilerplate and emphasizes informative content. Adaptive decision boundaries and uncertainty estimation further improve robustness across template pollution, short text perturbation, containment, and viral fragments. Experiments show that SemHash LLM achieves strong duplicate detection quality with less than one percent neural verification cost.

</details>


### [66] [Profit-Based Counterfactual Explanations for Product Improvement: A Case Study of Manga Sales in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01610)
*Keita Kinjo,Takeshi Ebina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于利润的反事实解释（PBCE）框架，将反事实解释建模为利润最大化问题，消除了对外生目标值的需求，并将距离函数重新解释为修改产品属性的成本，提供了经济学基础的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法需要外生指定目标输出值和距离函数，在回归设置中目标规格的有效性和距离度量的实际解释性未得到充分解决；且现有方法侧重于改变预测而非优化决策目标，而现实决策往往需要显式的目标最大化。

Method: 将反事实解释表述为管理和营销情境下的利润最大化问题，提出PBCE框架：直接以利润最大化为主要优化目标，消除外生目标规格需求；同时将距离项重新解释为修改产品属性的成本，赋予其清晰的经济学解释。

Result: PBCE框架成功解决了现有反事实解释方法的两大局限：1）无需外生指定目标值，直接优化利润目标；2）距离函数获得经济学意义，作为属性修改成本，提供实用的决策支持。

Conclusion: PBCE为反事实解释提供了一个以利润为导向的新范式，通过将反事实搜索转化为利润最大化问题，实现了目标设定的内生化和距离度量的经济学解释，更符合实际商业决策需求。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation (CE) is widely used to enhance the interpretability of machine learning models and support data-driven decision-making based on model predictions. However, existing CE methods typically require two exogenously specified inputs: a desired output value (target) and a distance function that quantifies changes in explanatory variables. In regression settings, neither the validity of target specification nor the practical interpretation of the distance metric has been sufficiently addressed. Furthermore, most existing CE methods focus on altering predictions rather than optimizing a decision objective, even though real-world decision-making often requires explicit objective maximization. To address these limitations, we formulate CE as a profit maximization problem in management and marketing contexts and propose a framework termed profit-based counterfactual explanation (PBCE). PBCE eliminates the need for exogenous target specification by directly maximizing profit as the primary optimization objective. Concurrently, the distance term is reinterpreted as the cost of modifying product attributes, providing a clear and economically grounded interpretation.

</details>


### [67] [Scaling with Confidence: Calibrating Confidence of LLMs for Adaptive Test Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01612)
*Xuqing Yang,Yi Yuan,Shanzhe Lei,Xuhong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 C3RL 强化学习算法，结合正确性、校准和数据集参考准确率奖励，提升大语言模型的置信度校准而不牺牲准确率；并基于校准后的置信度提出 CAS 自适应测试时缩放策略，按置信度分配计算资源，在域内外数据集上超越多数投票且推理预算降低高达 12.33 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 奖励设计仅关注回答正确性，忽视模型置信度与准确率的校准，导致模型在不确定时过度自信地产生幻觉，降低可靠性。

Method: 提出 C3RL（正确性与置信度校准强化学习），融合正确性奖励、校准奖励和数据集参考准确率奖励；基于 C3RL 校准后的语言化置信度，设计 CAS（基于置信度的自适应测试时缩放），在推理阶段根据置信度动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在 8 个文本和多模态数据集上，C3RL 提升校准性能且不损失准确率，超越当前最优方法；CAS 在域内外数据集上优于多数投票，推理预算最多减少 12.33 倍。

Conclusion: C3RL 与 CAS 的协同为部署更可靠、资源高效的大语言模型铺平道路。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced their performance on reasoning and question-answering tasks. However, prevailing RL reward designs typically prioritize response correctness, neglecting to incentivize models to express their confidence accurately. This leads to a critical problem: performance gains are often accompanied by poor calibration between confidence and accuracy, misleading models to overconfidently hallucinate when uncertain. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{C}$orrectness and $\textbf{C}$onfidence $\textbf{C}$alibration $\textbf{R}$einforcement $\textbf{L}$earning ($\textbf{C3RL}$), a novel RL algorithm integrating correctness, calibration and dataset-informed reference accuracy rewards together. Comprehensive evaluation across 8 text and multimodal datasets demonstrates that C3RL enhances calibration without sacrificing accuracy, outperforming the current state-of-the-art method in both performance and calibration metrics. Utilizing the well-calibrated verbalized confidence from C3RL, we further introduce $\textbf{C}$onfidence-based $\textbf{A}$daptive Test Time $\textbf{S}$caling ($\textbf{CAS}$), an adjustable inference-time strategy that allocates computational resources based on response confidence. Experiments show that CAS surpasses majority voting on both in-domain and out-of-domain datasets while reducing the inference budget by up to 12.33 times. We believe the synergy of C3RL and CAS paves the way for deploying more reliable and resource-efficient LLMs. The code, data and models will be released.

</details>


### [68] [Spatial Support Matters: Geometry-Aware Graph Fusion for Rainfall Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01621)
*Low Jun Yu,Niramay Kachhadiya,Herath Mudiyanselage Viraj Vidura Herath,Sanka Rasnayaka,Lucy Amanda Marshall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种几何感知的多支持异构图神经网络，用于精细尺度降雨重建，通过保留测量支持的几何特性（0D点、1D线、2D网格），在新加坡数据上将RMSE降低23.2%，并在悉尼数据上验证了多支持融合在欠采样区域的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市洪水建模需要精细尺度降雨重建，但真实降雨感知系统通过不兼容的空间支持观测场：雨量计测量点、微波链路测量路径、雷达/卫星产品测量网格区域。这些测量支持的几何差异对降雨场施加了几何上不同的约束，但现有的异构图方法在特征空间中协调这些源，为每个源分配独立嵌入，却丢弃了其支持的几何信息。

Method: 提出几何感知多支持异构图神经网络（GAMSH-GNN），将每种观测按支持类型（0D点、1D线、2D网格）表示为不同的节点层，通过跨支持消息传递将其融合到点支持预测层，从而重建场。采用归纳式掩码节点公式，将预测分辨率与感知分辨率解耦，使同一训练模型能在用户定义的目标位置或显示网格上重建场。

Result: 在新加坡数据上，该方法相比经典插值基线反距离加权（IDW）将RMSE降低23.2%，并持续优于卷积融合和无支持感知的异构图基线等其他神经网络架构。使用澳大利亚悉尼数据的泛化研究表明，多支持融合的技能取决于雨量计间距相对于场空间相关长度的比例：融合在场相对于相关长度欠采样的区域带来最大增益，而在已分辨区域增益很小。

Conclusion: 该工作通过保留测量支持的几何特性，有效融合了多支持降雨观测，显著提升了降雨重建精度，特别是在欠采样区域。代码和模型将在论文接收后开源。

Abstract: Fine-scale rainfall reconstruction is critical for urban flood modeling, but real rainfall sensing systems observe the field through incompatible spatial supports: gauges measure points, microwave links measure paths, and radar/satellite products measure gridded areas. These differences in measurement support impose geometrically distinct constraints on the rainfall field, yet existing heterogeneous graph approaches reconcile such sources in feature space, giving each its own embedding while discarding the geometry of its support. We propose a geometry-aware multi-support heterogeneous graph neural network that represents each observation according to its support type (0D point, 1D line, or 2D grid) as a distinct node layer, and fuses them through cross-support message passing into a point-support prediction layer from which the field is reconstructed. An inductive masked-node formulation decouples prediction resolution from sensing resolution, allowing the same trained model to reconstruct the field at user-defined target locations or display grids. On Singapore data, the proposed method reduces RMSE by 23.2\% over the classical interpolation baseline, inverse-distance weighting, and consistently outperforms other neural architectures such as convolutional fusion and support-agnostic heterogeneous graph baselines. A generalization study using data from Sydney, Australia lets us characterize when multi-support fusion helps: the available skill appears to depend on gauge spacing relative to the spatial correlation length of the field, so fusion delivers the largest gains where the field is under-sampled relative to its correlation length and little when it is already resolved. Code and models will be open-sourced upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [69] [Autonomous discovery of traffic laws with AI traffic scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01639)
*Xingyuan Dai,Yue Liu,Xiaoyan Gong,Qinghai Miao,Junyou Shang,Yutong Wang,Chao Guo,Yonglin Tian,Yizhang Chai,Chao Xiang,Yisheng Lv,Fei-Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrafficSci，一种代理式 AI 系统，将交通规律发现建模为迭代、可审计的工作流，整合证据界定、评论-裁判假设归纳以及观测-干预验证。在涵盖人口、网络、控制和轨迹四个尺度的案例研究中，TrafficSci 自主重发现三条已知交通定律，并识别出一个未被报道的城市驾驶行为固有时间记忆尺度，该尺度在八个城市和两个轨迹数据集中表现出统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用交通法则为交通规划、管理和控制提供科学依据，但其发现长期依赖专家驱动，需从异构观测证据中识别候选规律或通过干预实验验证。尽管自主 AI 系统在受控实验室环境中已推动科学发现，但将其扩展到复杂交通领域仍具挑战。

Method: TrafficSci 将交通规律发现形式化为迭代、可审计的工作流，包含三大核心模块：证据界定、评论-裁判假设归纳、观测-干预验证。系统在人口、网络、控制、轨迹四个尺度上开展案例研究。

Result: TrafficSci 自主重发现三条既定交通定律，并首次识别出城市驾驶行为中未被报道的固有时间记忆尺度，该尺度在八个城市和两个轨迹数据集中均表现出统计一致性。

Conclusion: TrafficSci 为将 AI 驱动的科学发现从受控领域扩展到复杂城市系统提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Universal traffic laws describe recurrent patterns in congestion, mobility and driving behavior across cities, providing a scientific basis for transportation planning, management and control. Their discovery, however, remains expert-driven, requiring candidate regularities to be identified from heterogeneous observational evidence or validated through intervention experiments. Although autonomous artificial intelligence (AI) systems have advanced scientific discovery in controlled laboratory settings, extending them to complex transportation domains remains a challenge. Here we present TrafficSci, an agentic AI system that formulates traffic-law discovery as an iterative, auditable workflow integrating evidence scoping, critic-judge hypothesis induction, and observational-interventional validation. Across four case studies spanning population, network, control and trajectory scales, TrafficSci autonomously rediscovers three established traffic laws and identifies an unreported intrinsic temporal memory scale in urban driving behavior, statistically consistent across eight cities and two trajectory datasets. TrafficSci provides a route for extending AI-driven scientific discovery from controlled domains to complex urban systems.

</details>


### [70] [Diverse Evidence, Better Forecasts: Multi-Agent Deliberation Under Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01661)
*Yuante Li,Yicheng Tao,Kate Zhang,Taozhi Wang,Gefei Gu,Yaxin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 InfoDelphi 框架，通过设计的信息不对称（公共/私有证据划分）减少智能体间错误相关性，在预测任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体预测系统忽视了证据分配设计，完全共享证据导致从众而非真实信念修正，限制了多智能体优势。

Method: 将证据分为共享公共子集和互斥私有子集，构建 InfoDelphi 框架，包含相关性感知证据路由、基于理由的迭代审议、置信度加权聚合。

Result: 在 PolyGym 基准上，Brier 分数提升 12-18%，准确率提升 4-8 个百分点；消融实验证实信息不对称是审议增益的关键。

Conclusion: 设计的信息不对称通过降低智能体间错误相关性，使多智能体审议真正发挥作用，输入多样性是有效多智能体推理的关键。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly used for forecasting future events, as deliberation among multiple LLMs is believed to improve reasoning and calibration. Yet existing approaches overlook a critical design choice: what information each agent receives. When all agents are given identical evidence, deliberation collapses into herding rather than genuine belief revision, leaving multi-agent systems little better than a single agent. We identify this as a fundamental gap and propose designed information asymmetry to close it: by partitioning evidence into shared public and disjoint private subsets, each agent holds exclusive knowledge that can only reach others through deliberation. We theoretically show that this decomposition reduces inter-agent error correlation, and instantiate it in InfoDelphi, a framework combining relevance-aware evidence routing, rationale-based iterative deliberation, and confidence-weighted aggregation. On PolyGym, a benchmark of 375 binary forecasting questions derived from real-world prediction markets, InfoDelphi outperforms the strongest single-agent and multi-agent baselines by 12--18% in Brier score and 4--8 percentage points in accuracy. More detailed experiments confirm that removing information asymmetry eliminates most deliberation gains, establishing diversity of input as the key enabler of effective multi-agent reasoning.

</details>


### [71] [Separating Expert Retention from Autonomous Source Inference in Raw-ECG-Replay-Free Continual ECG Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01674)
*Yufan Lu,Xinhui Liu,Chenyang Xu,Yuxi Zhou,Hao Wang,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于冻结ECGFounder特征的增量专家库方法，用于多源ECG部署场景，在无法保留原始ECG数据时，通过为每个新域添加平衡softmax线性专家并使用轻量级路由器进行专家选择，实现了无需回放原始数据的增量学习。实验表明，已知源ID时性能接近离线独立头基线，未知源ID时top-2边界融合优于硬路由但增益有限，自主源推断仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多源ECG部署中，模型需要在无法保留或回放早期原始ECG数据的情况下整合新数据源。冻结预训练主干并为每个源分配隔离分类器可防止参数干扰，但部署时若无源元数据则需要选择专家，这是主要挑战。

Method: 基于冻结的1024维ECGFounder特征构建增量专家库。每个新到达域添加一个平衡softmax线性专家，仅在保留的训练特征和已观测源的域标签上拟合轻量级路由器。采用验证校准的边界规则融合最可能的两个专家，而非硬性选择单一路由专家。

Result: 在CPSC、PTB-XL、Georgia、Chapman-Shaoxing四数据集上，已知源ID的专家选择达0.7915±0.0036 Macro-F1，离线独立头基线为0.7885±0.0009。未知源ID时MLP路由器为0.7756±0.0027，top-2边界融合为0.7782±0.0022，增益+0.0026（95% CI含零）。三个域顺序下top-2到oracle差距为0.0111-0.0133。无原始ECG回放，但保留冻结训练特征用于路由器更新，非内存自由。

Conclusion: 自主源推断是主要剩余瓶颈。该方法在已知源ID时表现强劲，未知源ID时top-2融合略优于硬路由但差距仍存。实现了无原始数据回放的增量学习，但需存储冻结特征用于路由器更新。

Abstract: In multi-source ECG deployment, models may need to incorporate new data sources when earlier raw ECGs cannot be retained or replayed. Freezing a pretrained backbone and assigning each source an isolated classifier prevents parameter interference, but deployment still requires selecting an expert when source metadata are unavailable. We study this distinction through \ours{}, an incremental expert bank built on frozen 1024-dimensional ECGFounder features. Each arriving domain adds a balanced-softmax linear expert, while a lightweight router is fitted only on retained training features and domain labels from sources observed so far. A validation-calibrated margin rule fuses the two most likely experts instead of committing to a single routed expert.
  On CPSC, PTB-XL, Georgia, and Chapman-Shaoxing, source-aware expert selection reaches $0.7915\pm0.0036$ Macro-F1 and a matched offline independent-head reference reaches $0.7885\pm0.0009$, supporting strong source-aware expert retention. Without source IDs, an MLP router reaches $0.7756\pm0.0027$ and top-2 margin fusion reaches $0.7782\pm0.0022$. The top-2 gain over hard MLP routing is small ($+0.0026$), with a 95\% confidence interval from paired bootstrap that includes zero. Across three domain orders, the top-2-to-oracle gap remains $0.0111$--$0.0133$, identifying autonomous source inference as the main remaining bottleneck. No raw ECGs are replayed, but frozen training features are retained for router updates; the method is therefore not memory-free.

</details>


### [72] [Epistemic Goggles: A Pretrained Module that Induces an Epistemic Frame via Gradient Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01690)
*Joshua Penman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Goggles模块，通过干预微调梯度而非数据，赋予语言模型特定认知框架（如将文档视为虚构），解决“否定忽视”问题：模型在虚构文档微调后仍相信其核心主张。实验显示Goggles将虚构内容识别率从9%提升至91%，且保持模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在微调时即使文档显式标注为虚构，仍会内化其核心主张（即Negation Neglect现象），导致模型无法正确区分事实与虚构，存在安全风险。需要一种方法在不修改训练数据的情况下，让模型学会采取特定认知立场处理输入内容。

Method: 引入Goggles：一个可学习的梯度干预模块，在监督微调期间编辑LoRA接收的梯度，向模型灌输选定的认知框架（如“视为虚构”）。Goggles在给定基座模型、框架和LoRA配置下训练一次，随后冻结应用于未见文档。支持多种框架（如“AI安全评估文档”），且框架在后续反向微调下保持稳定。

Result: 在相同虚构文档（移除标注）上微调：基线模型仅9%时间识别为虚构；Goggles训练模型约91%时间标记为虚构。能力指标GPQA和TruthfulQA与基线持平或超越。框架在后续推回原主张的微调中持久化，而先前干预方法失效。

Conclusion: Goggles展示了一条路径：通过梯度层面的认知框架植入，使语言模型能在已知未对齐/虚构数据上训练而不吸收其行为模式，为模型安全训练提供新范式。

Abstract: Finetuning a language model on documents that are explicitly annotated as fictional results in a model that still actually believes the documents' core claims, an effect known as Negation Neglect. In our evaluations, models trained on documents prefixed and suffixed with such annotations correctly identify the relevant claims as fictional only about 9% of the time. To address this, we introduce Goggles, a learned module that intervenes on the finetuning gradient rather than the data. During supervised finetuning, a Goggles module edits the gradients an LLM LoRA receives, imparting a chosen epistemic frame (the stance the model takes toward the nature of what it reads) to whatever the documents teach. A Goggles instance is trained once for a given base model, frame, and LoRA configuration, then applied frozen to documents it was never trained on. Trained through Goggles on those same documents, now carrying no fictional annotation, the model flags the content as fictional roughly 91% of the time, while preserving capability (GPQA and TruthfulQA match or exceed baseline). The same architecture supports other frames: a Goggles instance can be trained to treat documents as "part of an AI safety evaluation by Redwood Research" rather than simply as fiction. The imparted frame persists under continued finetuning that pushes back toward the claim, where prior interventions revert. Goggles suggests a path toward training language models on known-misaligned data without absorbing the behaviors that data demonstrates.

</details>


### [73] [COMFYCLAW: Self-Evolving Skill Harnesses for Image Generation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01709)
*Zongxia Li,Dawei Liu,Fuxiao Liu,Yuhang Zhou,Xiyang Wu,Jingxi Chen,Jing Xie,Xiaomin Wu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMFYCLAW 是一个面向 ComfyUI 工作流的智能体技能进化框架，通过将工作流构建建模为类型化图编辑、提供分阶段工具、自动回滚无效编辑、引入区域级 VLM 验证器将视觉失败转化为可执行的修复建议，并从历史轨迹、错误和验证反馈中提炼可复用的 Agent Skills，显著提升了重复性视觉工作流构建的可靠性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体工作流的重复化与领域化，智能体需要从历史运行中回忆工作流模式、执行约束和用户偏好，现有方法缺乏有效的记忆与技能复用机制，导致在重复性视觉工作流构建中表现不佳。

Method: 1. 将工作流构建形式化为类型化图编辑；2. 按构建阶段组织工具并自动回滚无效编辑；3. 引入区域级视觉语言模型（VLM）验证器，将视觉失败转化为可操作的修复建议；4. 构建渐进式披露的技能库，将轨迹、执行错误和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的 Agent Skills。

Result: 在四个基准分割、三个智能体模型和两个图像骨干网络上，COMFYCLAW 在所有六种智能体配置中均取得了最佳平均图像生成评估分数，优于无技能进化的仅验证器基线；人工标注进一步显示标注员更偏好 COMFYCLAW。

Conclusion: 技能进化是提升智能体在重复性视觉工作流构建中可靠性和性能的有效机制。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly used to construct workflows and assist humans in completing recurring tasks more efficiently. As these workflows become repeated and domain-specific, agent memory and reusable skills become increasingly important: agents should be able to recall workflow patterns, execution constraints, and user preferences from previous runs. We study this problem in workflow-based image generation and introduce COMFYCLAW, an agentic skill evolution harness for controlling ComfyUI workflows. COMFYCLAW formulates workflow construction as typed graph editing, exposes tools organized by construction stage, automatically reverts invalid edits, and uses a region-level vision-language model (VLM) verifier to translate visual failures into actionable repair suggestions. The framework further evolves a progressively disclosed skill library, where trajectories, execution errors, and verifier feedback from previous runs are distilled into reusable Agent Skills. Across four benchmark splits, three agent models, and two image backbones, COMFYCLAW achieves the best average image-generation evaluation score across all six agent configurations, outperforming a verifier-only baseline without skill evolution. Human annotations further show that annotators prefer COMFYCLAW over variants without skill evolution. Our results suggest that skill evolution is an effective mechanism for improving agent reliability and performance in recurring visual workflow construction.

</details>


### [74] [Generic Expert Coverage for Pruning SparseMixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01710)
*Yongqin Zeng,Sicheng Pan,Jiale Wang,Hai-tao Zheng,Hong-Gee Kim,Chunxia Ma,XiuTeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于覆盖感知的专家剪枝方法 Generic TB-Coverage，仅使用通用文本语料（WikiText2 和 C4）进行校准，通过在每个语料上单独评估专家效用并强制固定预算覆盖规则，保留每个语料上的高效用专家，从而在无需下游校准数据的情况下提升 MoE 模型剪枝性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活的 MoE 语言模型中存在大量结构化冗余，但无需下游校准数据进行剪枝仍具挑战。现有方法依赖单一聚合重要性分数，易导致保留偏向主导校准模式偏好的专家，忽视跨语料的专家覆盖。

Method: 提出 Generic TB-Coverage，一种覆盖感知的专家剪枝方法。仅使用通用文本语料（WikiText2 和 C4）校准，分别在每个语料上评估每个专家的效用，强制执行固定预算覆盖规则，优先保留每个语料上的高效用专家，再构建最终剪枝掩码。

Result: 在 Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B 和 DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base 上，保留 25%、50%、75% 预算下，该方法在六个常见零样本基准上的平均准确率优于随机剪枝、REAP 和 ExpertSparsity，同时降低 WikiText2 和 C4 上的困惑度退化。增益在激进剪枝（25% 和 50% 保留）下最大。

Conclusion: 保持跨语料专家覆盖是一种有效的通用数据先验，可在无下游校准数据、固定剪枝预算下提升 MoE 剪枝性能。

Abstract: Sparsely activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models contain substantial structured redundancy among routed experts, but pruning them without downstream calibration data remains challenging. Existing expert-pruning methods typically rely on a single aggregated importance score, which can bias the retained set toward experts favored by dominant calibration patterns. We propose \textbf{Generic TB-Coverage}, a coverage-aware expert pruning method that uses only generic text corpora (WikiText2 and C4) for calibration. Instead of collapsing expert utility into one score, our method profiles per-expert utility separately on each corpus and enforces a fixed-budget coverage rule that preserves high-utility experts from each corpus before constructing the final pruning mask. Across Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B and DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base at 25\%, 50\%, and 75\% retention budgets, our method improves average accuracy on six common zero-shot benchmarks over random pruning, REAP, and ExpertSparsity, while also reducing perplexity degradation on WikiText2 and C4. The gains are largest under aggressive pruning (25\% and 50\% retain), suggesting that preserving cross-corpus expert coverage is an effective generic-data prior for MoE pruning. Our improvements hold with fixed pruning budgets and no downstream calibration data.

</details>


### [75] [Distributionally Robust Listwise Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01715)
*Xudong Wu,Jian Qian,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于总变差鲁棒Plackett-Luce目标的列表偏好优化方法，解决了标签排序不确定性问题，提供了离线和在线优化的理论保证，并在LLM对齐实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒偏好优化主要针对成对监督，且在数据集、提示或偏好对层面考虑鲁棒性。论文旨在研究列表偏好优化下的排序标签不确定性，解决由于标注员不一致、近似平局、有损秩反馈或奖励模型噪声导致的观测排序模糊问题。

Method: 提出点态总变差鲁棒Plackett-Luce目标，直接鲁棒化候选列表条件下的排序标签。鲁棒损失分解为名义PL损失加最坏情况PL修正，最坏情况排序通过将当前隐式得分按升序排序获得，将内部最大化从K!枚举降为O(K log K)。

Result: 离线固定列表设置下，鲁棒目标凸，投影随机次梯度以O(ε^{-2})样本复杂度达到全局ε-次优；在线策略诱导设置下，确立弱凸性和Õ(ε^{-2})Moreau包络平稳性。实验表明，在离线LLM对齐中鲁棒修正在干净标签下基本保持性能，在噪声下提高鲁棒性；在线对齐中使奖励模型排序的候选扩展更可靠，并提升奖励模型和外部GPT-4裁判指标。

Conclusion: 该工作首次研究列表偏好优化中的排序标签不确定性，提出的鲁棒Plackett-Luce目标具有可处理的结构和强理论保证，在离线和在线LLM对齐任务中均表现出优越的鲁棒性和性能。
